field strengths of the supergravity theories and generalise (
5.8
) which exhibits only a 3-form coupling. We hope to report on such a development in the future.

Fundamental String

A direct computation using (

\displaystyle\sigma^{rs}=h^{-{1\over 2}}\big(-\langle\eta^{r},\lambda^{s}\rangle+\langle\lambda^{r},\eta^{s}\rangle\big)\penalty\ ,\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\

(A.2)

\displaystyle\omega^{rs}=h^{-{1\over 2}}\big(\langle\eta^{r},\lambda^{s}\rangle+\langle\lambda^{r},\eta^{s}\rangle\big)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}+{1\over 2}h^{-{1\over 2}}\big(-\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{s}\rangle+\langle\lambda^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{s}\rangle\big)e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\penalty\ ,

(A.3)

\displaystyle\pi^{rs}={1\over 2}h^{-{1\over 2}}\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{s}\rangle\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}+{1\over 2}h^{-{1\over 2}}\langle\lambda^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{s}\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\penalty\ ,

(A.4)

\displaystyle\zeta^{rs}={1\over 2}h^{-{1\over 2}}\big(\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{s}\rangle+\langle\lambda^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{s}\rangle\big)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

(A.5)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+{1\over 4!}h^{-{1\over 2}}\big(-\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ijk\ell}\lambda^{s}\rangle+\langle\lambda^{r},\Gamma_{ijk\ell}\eta^{s}\rangle\big)e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}\wedge e^{\ell}\penalty\ ,\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+{1\over 4!}h^{-{1\over 2}}\langle\lambda^{r},\Gamma_{ijk\ell}\lambda^{s}\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}\wedge e^{\ell}\penalty\ ,

where

are the transverse directions of the string and
(e^{0},e^{5},e^{i})
is a pseudo-orthonormal frame of the

The remaining form bilinears

Choosing the worldvolume and transverse directions of the IIB fundamental string as in the IIA case, a direct computation using (

\displaystyle k^{rs}=h^{-{1\over 2}}\langle\eta^{r},\eta^{s}\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})+h^{-{1\over 2}}\langle\lambda^{r},\lambda^{s}\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\penalty\ ,

Form bilinears of D-branes

Using the expression for the Killing spinors of the D0-brane (
6.1
), one finds that the non-vanishing from bilinears of the solution are

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{rs}=-2h^{-{1\over 4}}\,\langle\eta^{r},\eta^{s}\rangle\penalty\ ,\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ k^{rs}=2h^{-{1\over 4}}\,\langle\eta^{r},\eta^{s}\rangle\,e^{0}\penalty\ ,

\displaystyle\pi^{rs}=2h^{-{1\over 4}}\,\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{i}\Gamma_{11}\eta^{s}\rangle\,e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{i}+h^{-{1\over 4}}\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{s}\rangle e^{0}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\penalty\ ,

(B.5)

\displaystyle\tilde{\pi}^{rs}=-h^{-{1\over 4}}\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\Gamma_{11}\eta^{s}\rangle\,e^{5}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}+{1\over 3}h^{-{1\over 4}}\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ijk}\eta^{s}\rangle e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}\penalty\ ,

(B.6)

\displaystyle\zeta^{rs}=-h^{-{1\over 4}}\,\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ij}\Gamma_{11}\eta^{s}\rangle\,e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}+{1\over 3}h^{-{1\over 4}}\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ijk}\eta^{s}\rangle\,e^{0}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}\penalty\ ,

\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}^{rs}=-{1\over 3}h^{-{1\over 4}}\,\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ijk}\Gamma_{11}\eta^{s}\rangle\,e^{5}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}-{2\over 4!}h^{-{1\over 4}}\,\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{4}}\eta^{s}\rangle e^{i_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{i_{4}}\penalty\ ,

\displaystyle+{h^{-\frac{1}{4}}\over 3}(-\mathrm{Re}\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{abc}\eta^{2s}\rangle_{D}+\mathrm{Re}\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{abc}\lambda^{2s}\rangle_{D})\,e^{3}\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{a}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{c}

B.2
Form bilinears of IIB D-branes

As for the common sector IIB branes, all the bilinears below are manifestly real. In particular,
k^{(2)},\pi^{(2)}
and
\tau^{(2)}
have been replaced with
ik^{(2)},i\pi^{(2)}
and
i\tau^{(2)}
, respectively.

Using the Killing spinors (
7.3
), one can easily compute
the form bilinears of the D-string background to find

\displaystyle k^{rs}=2h^{-{1\over 4}}(\langle\eta^{r},\eta^{s}\rangle+\langle\lambda^{r},\lambda^{s}\rangle)e^{0}+2h^{-{1\over 4}}(-\langle\eta^{r},\eta^{s}\rangle+\langle\lambda^{r},\lambda^{s}\rangle)e^{5}\penalty\ ,

B.2.3
D3-brane

(B.169)

\displaystyle k^{(1)rs}=4h^{-{1\over 4}}\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{i}\penalty\ ,

(B.170)

\displaystyle\pi^{rs}

\displaystyle=h^{-{1\over 4}}\Big(2\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

\displaystyle+2\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

\displaystyle-2\left(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{4}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

\displaystyle-2\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{9}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

\displaystyle-4\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\eta^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{9}

\displaystyle+4\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{9}

\displaystyle+4\left(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{4}

\displaystyle+4\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{9}\Big)\penalty\ ,

(B.171)

\displaystyle\pi^{(1)rs}

\displaystyle=h^{-{1\over 4}}\Big(-4\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{i}

\displaystyle-4\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{i}

\displaystyle+4\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{9}\wedge e^{i}

\displaystyle-4\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{9}\wedge e^{i}

\displaystyle-4\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{i}

\displaystyle+4\left(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{4}\wedge e^{9}\wedge e^{i}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}\Big)\penalty\ ,

(B.172)

\displaystyle\tau^{rs}

\displaystyle=\frac{h^{-{1\over 4}}}{6}\Big(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{4}}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})+\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{4}}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})

\displaystyle-\left(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{4}}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{4}}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{4}

\displaystyle-\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{4}}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{4}}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{9}\Big)\wedge e^{i_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{i_{4}}

\displaystyle-2h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{9}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

\displaystyle+2h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{9}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

\displaystyle+2h^{-{1\over 4}}\left(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}

\displaystyle+2h^{-{1\over 4}}\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\lambda^{1s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ij}\eta^{1s}\right\rangle\right)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{9}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\penalty\ ,

(B.173)

\displaystyle\tau^{(1)rs}

\displaystyle=-4h^{-{1\over 4}}\left(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{9}\wedge e^{i}

\displaystyle\frac{2h^{-{1\over 4}}}{3}\Big(-\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{4}-\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{4}

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}-e^{5})\wedge e^{9}-\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle(e^{0}+e^{5})\wedge e^{9}

\displaystyle-\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{0}\wedge e^{5}

\displaystyle+\left(\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle-\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{ijk}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{4}\wedge e^{9}\Big)\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}

\displaystyle+\frac{4h^{-{1\over 4}}}{5!}\left(\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{5}}\lambda^{2s}\right\rangle+\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\lambda^{1r},\Gamma_{i_{1}\dots i_{5}}\eta^{2s}\right\rangle\right)e^{i_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{i_{5}}\penalty\ ,

(B.174)

where
i,j,k=1,6,2,7,3,8
and
(e^{0},e^{5},e^{4},e^{9})
is a pseudo-orthonormal frame of the D3-brane metric (
4.28
) for
p=3
.
The
k^{(2)},\pi^{(2)}
, and
\tau^{(2)}
form bilinears can be obtained from
k,\pi
, and
\tau
, and the
k^{(3)},\pi^{(3)}
, and
\tau^{(3)}
form bilinears can be obtained from
k^{(1)},\pi^{(1)}
, and
\tau^{(1)}
after replacing
\operatorname{Re}
and
\operatorname{Im}
with
\operatorname{Im}
and
-\operatorname{Re}
, respectively.

B.2.4
D7-brane

Using the Killing spinors (
7.28
), one can compute the form bilinears of the D7-brane to find

k^{rs}=4h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{a}\eta^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a}\penalty\ ,

(B.175)

\pi^{rs}=-4h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{a}\eta^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a}\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{9}+\frac{2}{3}h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{abc}\eta^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{c}\penalty\ ,

(B.176)

\displaystyle\tau^{rs}=-\frac{2}{3}h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{abc}\eta^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{c}\wedge e^{4}\wedge e^{9}

(B.177)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{4}{5!}h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{a_{1}\dots a_{5}}\eta^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{a_{5}}\penalty\ ,

(B.178)

where
a,b,c=0,5,1,6,2,7,3,8
and
(e^{a},e^{4},e^{9})
is a pseudo-orthonormal frame of the metric (
4.28
) for
p=7
.
The form bilinears
k^{(2)},\pi^{(2)}
and
\tau^{(2)}
can be obtained from
k
,
\pi
and
\tau
after replacing
\operatorname{Re}
and
\operatorname{Im}
with
\operatorname{Im}
and
-\operatorname{Re}
, respectively, in all the above expressions.

The rest bilinears are given by

k^{(3)rs}=4h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{r},\lambda^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{4}+4h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{r},\lambda^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{9}\penalty\ ,

(B.179)

\pi^{(3)rs}=2h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ab}\lambda^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{4}+2h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{ab}\lambda^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{9}\penalty\ ,

(B.180)

\displaystyle\tau^{(3)rs}=\frac{1}{6}h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Re}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{a_{1}\dots a_{4}}\lambda^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{a_{4}}\wedge e^{4}

(B.181)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{6}h^{-{1\over 4}}\operatorname{Im}\left\langle\eta^{r},\Gamma_{a_{1}\dots a_{4}}\lambda^{s}\right\rangle_{D}e^{a_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{a_{4}}\wedge e^{9}\penalty\ .

(B.182)

Again the bilinears
k^{(1)},\pi^{(1)}
and
\tau^{(1)}
can be derived from
k^{(3)},\pi^{(3)}
and
\tau^{(3)}
after replacing
\operatorname{Re}
and
\operatorname{Im}
with
\operatorname{Im}
and
-\operatorname{Re}
, respectively, in all three expressions above.
